<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jjmaxwell4.com/writing/things-to-know-when-you-get-cancer/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Things to know when you get cancer</span><span class='zh'>患癌时应知道的事——2026 版</span></a><span class="dom">jjmaxwell4.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 2026 edition updates what cancer patients need to know, highlighting advances in treatment options and decision-making.</span><span class='zh'>2026 版更新了癌症患者应了解的要点，着重介绍治疗选项的进展与决策过程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 2026 edition updates what cancer patients need to know, highlighting advances in treatment options and decision-making. It also flags access to care, side-effect management, and the role of digital health resources.</span><span class='zh'>2026 版更新了癌症患者应了解的要点，着重介绍治疗选项的进展与决策过程。还提到就医获取、副作用管理，以及数字健康资源的作用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://deepmind.google/models/gemini/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Argon</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Argon 模型</span></a><span class="dom">deepmind.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Argon is the latest in Google&#x27;s Gemini lineup, offering stronger reasoning, higher factual accuracy, and more reliable tool usage across tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Argon 是谷歌 Gemini 系列的最新版本，提升了推理能力、事实准确性和跨任务的工具使用稳定性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Argon is the latest in Google&#x27;s Gemini lineup, offering stronger reasoning, higher factual accuracy, and more reliable tool usage across tasks. It signals continued maturation of high-end multilingual agents aimed at developers and enterprises, with an emphasis on safety, efficiency, and real-world usefulness.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Argon 是谷歌 Gemini 系列的最新版本，提升了推理能力、事实准确性和跨任务的工具使用稳定性。该发布体现高端多语言代理的持续成熟，重点放在安全性、效率与实际应用价值。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://nonfunctional.substack.com/p/passkeys-are-a-trap-do-not-use-them" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=nonfunctional.substack.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>nonfunctional.substack.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nonfunctional.substack.com/p/passkeys-are-a-trap-do-not-use-them" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Passkeys are a trap</span><span class='zh'>Passkeys 是陷阱。请不要使用它们。</span></a><span class="dom">nonfunctional.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A provocative stance arguing passkeys introduce risk, friction, or vendor lock-in rather than solving security problems.</span><span class='zh'>这篇观点认为 Passkeys 并未真正解决安全问题，反而带来新的风险、使用摩擦和厂商锁定。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A provocative stance arguing passkeys introduce risk, friction, or vendor lock-in rather than solving security problems. It cautions readers to consider trade-offs and to explore alternatives like hardware-backed keys or password managers. The piece invites debate about modern authentication design.</span><span class='zh'>这篇观点认为 Passkeys 并未真正解决安全问题，反而带来新的风险、使用摩擦和厂商锁定。强调权衡取舍并探讨硬件密钥、密码管理工具等替代方案，鼓励对现代认证设计的讨论。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>1093</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048312' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>559</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50122800" data-id="50122800" data-tags="Security|AI" data-score="1044" data-comments="386" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122800" data-time="1792176959">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.anthropic.com/research/ecdsa-curve-origins" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=anthropic.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>anthropic.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/research/ecdsa-curve-origins" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude reveals ECDSA curve origins</span><span class='zh'>Claude 揭示 ECDSA 曲线的来源</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Claude-based analysis traced the provenance of ECDSA elliptic-curve parameters, revealing how the seeds and generation choices were produced and documented.</span><span class='zh'>使用 Claude 的分析追溯了 ECDSA 椭圆曲线参数的生成来源，揭示了用于生成曲线的种子与选择的记录方式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Claude-based analysis traced the provenance of ECDSA elliptic-curve parameters, revealing how the seeds and generation choices were produced and documented. That provenance matters because the trustworthiness and backdoor risk of standard curves depend on transparent, auditable generation procedures.</span><span class='zh'>使用 Claude 的分析追溯了 ECDSA 椭圆曲线参数的生成来源，揭示了用于生成曲线的种子与选择的记录方式。曲线生成的可追溯性很重要，直接关系到标准曲线是否值得信任以及是否存在后门风险。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>1044</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122800' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>386</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50091788" data-id="50091788" data-tags="Security|AI" data-score="945" data-comments="616" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50091788" data-time="1791999923">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.csoonline.com/article/4234854/ai-agents-arent-going-rogue-frontier-labs-are-skipping-security-basics.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.csoonline.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/4234854-0-28632700-1792004597-shutterstock_2472843149.jpg?quality=50&amp;strip=all&amp;w=1024' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='csoonline.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.csoonline.com/article/4234854/ai-agents-arent-going-rogue-frontier-labs-are-skipping-security-basics.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Containment and monitoring failures are to blame for AI hacking reports: experts</span><span class='zh'>专家称AI黑客报道源于封控与监控失败</span></a><span class="dom">csoonline.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Security experts attribute AI hacking reports to containment and monitoring failures rather than rogue AI behavior.</span><span class='zh'>安全专家将 AI 黑客报道归因于封控与监控失败，而非 AI 自主失控。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Security experts attribute AI hacking reports to containment and monitoring failures rather than rogue AI behavior. The piece underscores the need for stronger containment, better monitoring, and robust incident-response practices when deploying AI agents.</span><span class='zh'>安全专家将 AI 黑客报道归因于封控与监控失败，而非 AI 自主失控。文章强调在部署 AI 代理时需加强封控、改进监控，并建立健全的事件响应机制。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>945</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50091788' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>616</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50050553" data-id="50050553" data-tags="Health" data-score="934" data-comments="658" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50050553" data-time="1791771714">
<a class="thumb" href="https://mapwriting.substack.com/p/dentists" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#4ade80' data-tag='Health'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=mapwriting.substack.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>mapwriting.substack.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mapwriting.substack.com/p/dentists" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Do dentists know what they&#x27;re doing?</span><span class='zh'>牙医真的知道自己在做什么吗？</span></a><span class="dom">mapwriting.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece questions variability in dental diagnosis and treatment, highlighting instances of over-treatment, opaque pricing, and inconsistent standards across practitioners.</span><span class='zh'>文章质疑牙科诊断与治疗的差异性，指出过度治疗、价格不透明和不同医生之间标准不一的现象。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece questions variability in dental diagnosis and treatment, highlighting instances of over-treatment, opaque pricing, and inconsistent standards across practitioners. It urges patients to seek second opinions and clinicians to adopt clearer guidelines and transparency to reduce unnecessary procedures.</span><span class='zh'>文章质疑牙科诊断与治疗的差异性，指出过度治疗、价格不透明和不同医生之间标准不一的现象。作者建议患者寻求第二诊疗意见，并呼吁行业采纳更清晰的指南与透明度以减少不必要的手术。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/cyber-security/unprecedented-cyberattack-cripples-supermarket-fridges-across-europe-as-hackers-activate-automatic-defrosting-delivery-and-store-prices-also-impacted-by-incident" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hackers activate automatic defrosting in supermarket fridges across Europe</span><span class='zh'>黑客在欧洲多国超市冷藏设备上启用自动除霜</span></a><span class="dom">tomshardware.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A coordinated cyberattack triggered automatic defrosting across supermarket fridges in Europe, disrupting storage conditions and potentially impacting pricing and stock.</span><span class='zh'>一次跨国网络攻击促使欧洲多家超市的冷藏设备启用自动除霜，干扰了冷藏条件，可能影响库存与价格。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A coordinated cyberattack triggered automatic defrosting across supermarket fridges in Europe, disrupting storage conditions and potentially impacting pricing and stock. The incident underscores how IoT-enabled retail devices can be manipulated at scale, highlighting security gaps in supply chains and the importance of robust device hygiene.</span><span class='zh'>一次跨国网络攻击促使欧洲多家超市的冷藏设备启用自动除霜，干扰了冷藏条件，可能影响库存与价格。这一事件凸显了零售物联网设备的安全脆弱性，以及对供应链安全治理的迫切需求。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A heartwarming clip shows a woman who rescued an injured crow continuing to receive &#x27;thank-you gifts&#x27; from the birds, illustrating surprising reciprocity in wildlife behavior.</span><span class='zh'>一位救助受伤乌鸦的女子的视频记录了鸟儿持续给她送来‘谢礼’，揭示了人鸟关系中的意外互动和动物行为的趣味一面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A heartwarming clip shows a woman who rescued an injured crow continuing to receive &#x27;thank-you gifts&#x27; from the birds, illustrating surprising reciprocity in wildlife behavior. The video captures the quirks of animal gratitude and the human-animal bond that forms around care. A light note on how small acts can ripple through nature in endearing ways.</span><span class='zh'>一位救助受伤乌鸦的女子的视频记录了鸟儿持续给她送来‘谢礼’，揭示了人鸟关系中的意外互动和动物行为的趣味一面。这个温情故事体现了善举在自然界里可能引发的连锁反应。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://already-clean.goodfire.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Neural networks are clean</span><span class='zh'>神经网络是干净的</span></a><span class="dom">already-clean.goodfire.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Neural networks can be kept clean through careful data handling and privacy-preserving training, addressing concerns about data leakage.</span><span class='zh'>通过谨慎的数据处理与隐私保护训练，可以让神经网络保持“干净”，降低数据泄露的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Neural networks can be kept clean through careful data handling and privacy-preserving training, addressing concerns about data leakage.</span><span class='zh'>通过谨慎的数据处理与隐私保护训练，可以让神经网络保持“干净”，降低数据泄露的担忧。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/ledger-confirms-hardware-wallet-spy-implant-behind-crypto-thefts/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ledger confirms spy chip implant behind wallet crypto thefts</span><span class='zh'>Ledger 证实硬件钱包存在间谍芯片植入，涉多起加密盗窃</span></a><span class="dom">bleepingcomputer.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ledger confirms that spy-chip implants were behind some compromised hardware wallets tied to crypto thefts, underscoring ongoing supply-chain risk in crypto hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Ledger 证实部分硬件钱包被植入间谍芯片，导致加密盗窃事件暴露供应链脆弱性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ledger confirms that spy-chip implants were behind some compromised hardware wallets tied to crypto thefts, underscoring ongoing supply-chain risk in crypto hardware. The disclosure adds to a wary narrative around provenance, tamper-resistance, and firmware integrity in popular wallets.</span><span class='zh'>Ledger 证实部分硬件钱包被植入间谍芯片，导致加密盗窃事件暴露供应链脆弱性。此事凸显源头可追溯性、抗篡改能力与固件完整性在热销钱包中的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://yyhh.org/blog/2026/10/ai-is-a-tool/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI is a tool and using it well takes skill</span><span class='zh'>AI 是工具，善用它需要技巧</span></a><span class="dom">yyhh.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that AI is a tool and its value comes from how skillfully it is used, urging engineers to develop practical workflows, critical thinking, and governance.</span><span class='zh'>文章将 AI 视为工具，强调其价值取决于使用者的技能水平，呼吁建立实用工作流、批判性思维和治理机制以避免滥用与过度乐观。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that AI is a tool and its value comes from how skillfully it is used, urging engineers to develop practical workflows, critical thinking, and governance.</span><span class='zh'>文章将 AI 视为工具，强调其价值取决于使用者的技能水平，呼吁建立实用工作流、批判性思维和治理机制以避免滥用与过度乐观。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://royfish.dev/fingerprints-are-the-new-cookies" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Fingerprints are the new cookies</span><span class='zh'>指纹识别成为新的网络追踪手段</span></a><span class="dom">royfish.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Browser fingerprinting persists as a tracking technique despite cookie erosion, enabling unique combos of device attributes to identify users across sites.</span><span class='zh'>尽管 cookies 逐渐被边缘化，浏览器指纹仍作为一种持续的跟踪手段，通过设备属性的组合实现跨站点识别。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Browser fingerprinting persists as a tracking technique despite cookie erosion, enabling unique combos of device attributes to identify users across sites. It underscores the ongoing tension between usability and privacy online.</span><span class='zh'>尽管 cookies 逐渐被边缘化，浏览器指纹仍作为一种持续的跟踪手段，通过设备属性的组合实现跨站点识别。文章讨论隐私影响及可能的对策，包括反指纹技术与浏览器保护措施，强调在线可用性与隐私之间的持续张力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rust-lang/rust/issues/164250" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>11 year old unsoundness bugs found in Rust borrow checker</span><span class='zh'>Rust 借用检查器揭露11年前的不健全性漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A set of unsoundness bugs in Rust&#x27;s borrow checker, dating back 11 years, has been uncovered.</span><span class='zh'>揭示了 Rust 的借用检查器中存在的11年前的不健全性漏洞，凸显在复杂类型系统中长期保持内存安全的挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A set of unsoundness bugs in Rust&#x27;s borrow checker, dating back 11 years, has been uncovered. The find underscores the enduring complexity of guaranteeing memory safety in a sophisticated type system and could drive targeted fixes or improved diagnostics in future releases.</span><span class='zh'>揭示了 Rust 的借用检查器中存在的11年前的不健全性漏洞，凸显在复杂类型系统中长期保持内存安全的挑战。此发现可能推动针对性修复与改进诊断能力，影响未来版本的安全性保障。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/karpathy/status/2109361546505966046" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The gap in shared understanding of LLM capability is widening</span><span class='zh'>对大型语言模型能力的共识差距正在扩大</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Different communities—researchers, engineers, and users—hold divergent expectations about what LLMs can do.</span><span class='zh'>研究者、工程师和用户对 LLM 能力的认知正在拉开差距，易导致被炒作驱动的部署与政策不匹配。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Different communities—researchers, engineers, and users—hold divergent expectations about what LLMs can do. The widening gap risks hype-driven deployments and misaligned policy. The piece advocates clearer benchmarks, transparent capability statements, and shared evaluation standards.</span><span class='zh'>研究者、工程师和用户对 LLM 能力的认知正在拉开差距，易导致被炒作驱动的部署与政策不匹配。文章主张建立更清晰的基准、公开的能力陈述和统一的评估标准。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://prophitt.me/writing/a-modern-web-app-in-c" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Modern Web App in C</span><span class='zh'>用 C 构建现代 Web 应用</span></a><span class="dom">prophitt.me</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The article argues that modern web apps can be built in C, likely via WebAssembly, highlighting architecture, tooling, and performance trade-offs.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨如何用 C 构建现代 Web 应用，可能通过 WebAssembly 实现前端与后端的高性能协作，分析架构、工具链与性能取舍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The article argues that modern web apps can be built in C, likely via WebAssembly, highlighting architecture, tooling, and performance trade-offs. It challenges conventional language choices and invites reconsideration of full-stack approach.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨如何用 C 构建现代 Web 应用，可能通过 WebAssembly 实现前端与后端的高性能协作，分析架构、工具链与性能取舍。它挑战了传统的语言选择观，鼓励重新考虑全栈开发的路径。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sfstandard.com/2026/10/13/openai-engineer-arrested-alleged-bugging-executive-conference-room/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI engineer arrested in bugging of executive conference room</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 工程师因在高管会议室安装窃听装置被逮捕</span></a><span class="dom">sfstandard.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Federal authorities arrested a former OpenAI engineer over allegations that he planted a listening device in an executive conference room.</span><span class='zh'>前 OpenAI 工程师因涉嫌在高管会议室安装窃听装置被逮捕。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Federal authorities arrested a former OpenAI engineer over allegations that he planted a listening device in an executive conference room. The case underscores ongoing concerns about insider risk and corporate security in AI labs, especially given the sensitivity of leadership-level discussions. If proven, it could invite scrutiny of internal controls and monitoring policies in fast-moving tech companies.</span><span class='zh'>前 OpenAI 工程师因涉嫌在高管会议室安装窃听装置被逮捕。此案突显企业内部风险与安保的重要性，尤其在处理敏感策略和产品开发信息的 AI 研究机构。若指控成立，可能促使公司加强内部控制与员工监控政策。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author explains why they self-host key services instead of relying on cloud providers, focusing on privacy, control, and resilience. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.pv-magazine.com/2026/09/29/solar-surpasses-nuclear-power-globally-for-first-time/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Solar surpasses nuclear power globally for first time</span><span class='zh'>全球太阳能首次超过核电</span></a><span class="dom">pv-magazine.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Solar power has overtaken nuclear as the world&#x27;s largest source of electricity for the first time, marking a milestone in the shift to renewables.</span><span class='zh'>全球太阳能首次超过核电，成为全球最大的发电来源，标志着可再生能源转型的里程碑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Solar power has overtaken nuclear as the world&#x27;s largest source of electricity for the first time, marking a milestone in the shift to renewables. The growth reflects rapid installations, cheaper PV hardware, and supportive policies, while grid integration and storage remain challenges. The development underscores a decarbonization trend with wide implications for utilities and energy markets.</span><span class='zh'>全球太阳能首次超过核电，成为全球最大的发电来源，标志着可再生能源转型的里程碑。快速的装机增长、日益降低的光伏组件成本以及利好政策推动起作用，但电网接入与储能仍然是挑战。此趋势强化了去碳化的长期走向，对公用事业和能源市场具有广泛影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://scotthelme.co.uk/passkeys-are-not-a-trap/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Passkeys are not a trap</span><span class='zh'>Passkeys 不是陷阱</span></a><span class="dom">scotthelme.co.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Passkeys offer phishing-resistant, passwordless authentication and smoother sign-ins across devices.</span><span class='zh'>Passkeys 提供防钓鱼的无密码认证和跨设备的顺畅登录。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Passkeys offer phishing-resistant, passwordless authentication and smoother sign-ins across devices. The piece argues that concerns about risks are overstated and emphasizes device-bound trust with robust recovery options, supporting a broader shift to modern web authentication.</span><span class='zh'>Passkeys 提供防钓鱼的无密码认证和跨设备的顺畅登录。文章认为对其风险的担忧被高估，强调设备绑定的信任关系与可行的恢复机制，从而推动向现代化、无密码的网页认证过渡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://alexwang.ai/posts/associative-memory/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>You could have invented transformers from memory</span><span class='zh'>你本可以凭记忆发明 Transformer</span></a><span class="dom">alexwang.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post argues that transformers could have been derived from associative-memory principles and simple design choices, reframing attention as a form of content-addressable recall.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张可以从联想记忆的原理和若干简单设计中推导出 Transformer，把注意力机制视为一种基于内容的检索。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post argues that transformers could have been derived from associative-memory principles and simple design choices, reframing attention as a form of content-addressable recall. That perspective clarifies why transformers generalize well and suggests alternative conceptual paths that might have yielded the architecture earlier.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张可以从联想记忆的原理和若干简单设计中推导出 Transformer，把注意力机制视为一种基于内容的检索。这个视角解释了 Transformer 的良好泛化性，并暗示有其他概念路径可能更早带来相似架构。</span></p>

The revelation adds to the scrutiny around professional services firms and tax avoidance practices, with potential implications for audits, liability, and regulatory oversight.</span><span class='zh'>据英国调查称，德勤伪造文件以规避罗曼·阿布拉莫维奇财富的税务。这一披露凸显专业服务机构在税收规避中的争议，引发对审计责任、合规与监管监管的潜在影响的关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.mercor.com/research/labor-index/benchmark-map/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>90% of US working hours are spent on tasks not covered well by AI benchmarks</span><span class='zh'>美国90%工作时间花在AI基准覆盖不足的任务上</span></a><span class="dom">mercor.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New findings from Mercor&#x27;s labor-index benchmark map show that about 90% of US working hours involve tasks not well captured by existing AI benchmarks.</span><span class='zh'>Mercor 的劳动指数显示，美国约90%的工作时间涉及的任务并未被现有 AI 基准很好覆盖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New findings from Mercor&#x27;s labor-index benchmark map show that about 90% of US working hours involve tasks not well captured by existing AI benchmarks. This misalignment means AI metrics may overstate automation gains and underrepresent where humans still outpace automated tools. The result matters for product teams planning automation and policymakers evaluating AI impact on the workforce.</span><span class='zh'>Mercor 的劳动指数显示，美国约90%的工作时间涉及的任务并未被现有 AI 基准很好覆盖。这一错位可能导致对自动化收益的高估，并低估人类在关键工作中的优势。对需要部署自动化的团队和评估 AI 对劳动力影响的决策者来说，这一洞察意义重大。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/48195" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Codex CLI installs a persistent, self-updating background daemon by default</span><span class='zh'>Codex CLI 默认安装一个持久且自更新的后台守护进程</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Codex CLI now ships with a background daemon that persists and self-updates by default, raising concerns about user consent, security, and potential persistence.</span><span class='zh'>Codex CLI 现默认随附一个持续运行并自更新的后台守护进程，引发对用户同意、安全性及长期持久化的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Codex CLI now ships with a background daemon that persists and self-updates by default, raising concerns about user consent, security, and potential persistence. Critics warn this can create a form of &#x27;auto-update&#x27; risk and complicate auditing. This change could influence how developers package CLIs and how users trust automation.</span><span class='zh'>Codex CLI 现默认随附一个持续运行并自更新的后台守护进程，引发对用户同意、安全性及长期持久化的担忧。有人担心这类自动更新会带来可追溯性和审计难题。这一变动可能影响开发者在打包 CLI 时的做法以及用户对自动化工具的信任。</span></p>

Implication: multi-region resilience and fast failover are essential for IaaS users.</span><span class='zh'>Yandex Cloud 的第三个数据中心遭遇故障，凸显区域性云部署的脆弱性。事件考验客户对可用性和云厂商跨区域冗余/应急响应的期望。意义在于强调对 IaaS 用户而言，多区域容错和快速故障转移至关重要。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.dataforprogress.org/blog/2026/9/29/americans-social-activity-has-steeply-declined-in-the-past-30-years" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Americans&#x27; social activity has steeply declined in the past 30 years</span><span class='zh'>过去三十年美国人的社交活动显著下降</span></a><span class="dom">dataforprogress.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A data-driven analysis shows Americans&#x27; social activity has declined sharply over the last 30 years, with fewer in-person interactions and waning social capital.</span><span class='zh'>基于数据的分析显示，在过去的三十年里，美国人的社交活动显著下降，面对面互动减少，社群资本薄弱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A data-driven analysis shows Americans&#x27; social activity has declined sharply over the last 30 years, with fewer in-person interactions and waning social capital. The trend raises questions about mental health, community resilience, and how digital life shapes social behavior.</span><span class='zh'>基于数据的分析显示，在过去的三十年里，美国人的社交活动显著下降，面对面互动减少，社群资本薄弱。此趋势引发对心理健康、社区韧性以及数字生活如何塑造社交行为的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nyc.gov/main/click-to-cancel" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>NYC&#x27;s &quot;Click to Cancel&quot; rule is now in effect</span><span class='zh'>纽约市的“点选取消”规则现已生效</span></a><span class="dom">nyc.gov</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New York City&#x27;s click-to-cancel rule is now in effect, mandating easy unsubscribe flows for digital subscriptions.</span><span class='zh'>纽约市的点选取消规则正式生效，要求订阅服务提供商提供更便捷的取消路径和更清晰的条款。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New York City&#x27;s click-to-cancel rule is now in effect, mandating easy unsubscribe flows for digital subscriptions. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nature.com/articles/d41586-026-03266-x" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bleached corals thrive within days when touching healthy neighbour</span><span class='zh'>接触健康邻居后，白化珊瑚数日内快速恢复</span></a><span class="dom">nature.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New evidence shows bleached corals can recover within days when in contact with nearby healthy corals.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示，白化珊瑚在接触附近健康珊瑚时可在数日内恢复。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New evidence shows bleached corals can recover within days when in contact with nearby healthy corals. The interaction likely involves beneficial chemical signals or metabolite exchange that jump-starts recovery and resilience. The finding highlights potential reef restoration strategies that leverage proximity or graft-like approaches to accelerate recovery.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示，白化珊瑚在接触附近健康珊瑚时可在数日内恢复。这一现象可能来自健康珊瑚释放的有益信号分子或代谢物的转移，帮助受损珊瑚重新获得活性。此发现为珊瑚礁修复提供了新的思路，如通过近邻效应或嫁接等方法加速康复。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ICE plans a $100 million domestic surveillance program codenamed Nightmare, signaling a bold expansion of law-enforcement data-gathering at home.</span><span class='zh'>ICE 计划1亿美元的Nightmare 国内监控项目，标志着国内执法数据收集能力的重大扩张。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.androidauthority.com/google-auto-phone-number-verification-android-3723117/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google is silently linking your phone number across all signed-in accounts</span><span class='zh'>谷歌悄悄将你的电话号码链接到所有已登录账户</span></a><span class="dom">androidauthority.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google&#x27;s automatic phone-number verification appears to be linking a number across all accounts a user is signed into, potentially collapsing account separation and exposing contact information across profiles.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌的自动电话号码验证似乎把同一号码关联到用户所有已登录的账户上，可能破坏账户之间的隔离并让联系信息在多个资料间可见。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google&#x27;s automatic phone-number verification appears to be linking a number across all accounts a user is signed into, potentially collapsing account separation and exposing contact information across profiles. This behavior raises privacy concerns for people who use different accounts for work, personal use, or anonymity and expect phone numbers to remain account-specific.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌的自动电话号码验证似乎把同一号码关联到用户所有已登录的账户上，可能破坏账户之间的隔离并让联系信息在多个资料间可见。这种做法对同时使用工作、个人或匿名账户的人来说构成隐私隐患，因为他们通常期望电话号码保持与特定账户绑定。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/oct/15/israeli-firm-digital-ads-enabling-installation-of-zero-click-spyware-researchers-say" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Online ads weaponised to deliver ‘zero-click’ spyware, researchers say</span><span class='zh'>研究称 在线广告被武器化用于投放零点击间谍软件</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers reveal that online ads can deliver spyware without any user clicks by exploiting zero-click flaws.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员披露，某些在线广告可利用零点击漏洞在用户不点击的情况下安装间谍软件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers reveal that online ads can deliver spyware without any user clicks by exploiting zero-click flaws. The technique leverages the ad ecosystem to silently compromise devices, dramatically increasing reach and stealth. The finding underscores the need for stronger vetting of ad networks and better defenses in browsers and apps.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员披露，某些在线广告可利用零点击漏洞在用户不点击的情况下安装间谍软件。这种方法通过广告生态系统实现隐蔽渗透，显著扩大攻击覆盖面与隐蔽性。此发现凸显加强广告网络安全、以及浏览器和应用防护的紧迫性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/android/ndk/issues/2260" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AOSP Source Releases Are So Late Developers Disassemble Libraries to Report Bugs</span><span class='zh'>AOSP 源码发布太晚，开发者解构库以报告漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Late AOSP source releases are spurring developers to reverse-engineer libraries to report bugs, underscoring the tension between open-source transparency and practical debugging.</span><span class='zh'>AOSP 源码发布延迟，迫使开发者通过反汇编库来定位并报告漏洞，凸显开源透明度与实际调试之间的矛盾与挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Late AOSP source releases are spurring developers to reverse-engineer libraries to report bugs, underscoring the tension between open-source transparency and practical debugging.</span><span class='zh'>AOSP 源码发布延迟，迫使开发者通过反汇编库来定位并报告漏洞，凸显开源透明度与实际调试之间的矛盾与挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://citizenlab.ca/research/how-discover-tech-turned-targeted-ads-into-a-pipeline-for-spyware/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AdFection: Targeted ads turned into a pipeline for zero-click mercenary spyware</span><span class='zh'>AdFection：定向广告如何成为零点击就可投放雇佣型间谍软件的管道</span></a><span class="dom">citizenlab.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AdFection reveals how targeted advertising ecosystems can be repurposed as a pipeline for deploying mercenary spyware with zero-click delivery.</span><span class='zh'>AdFection 揭示定向广告生态如何被逆向利用，成为零点击就能投放雇佣型间谍软件的渠道。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AdFection reveals how targeted advertising ecosystems can be repurposed as a pipeline for deploying mercenary spyware with zero-click delivery. The investigation highlights privacy and security risks in adtech and how attackers leverage data and real-time bidding to enable covert surveillance.</span><span class='zh'>AdFection 揭示定向广告生态如何被逆向利用，成为零点击就能投放雇佣型间谍软件的渠道。调查凸显广告技术中的隐私与安全风险，以及攻击者如何利用数据与实时竞价实现隐蔽监控。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://claude.dev/blog/solving-code-review-at-anthropic/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://claude.dev/blog/solving-code-review-at-anthropic/og.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='claude.dev' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://claude.dev/blog/solving-code-review-at-anthropic/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How the Claude Code team does code review</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 团队如何进行代码审查</span></a><span class="dom">claude.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Claude Code team describes a hybrid code-review workflow that combines automated checks from Claude with human reviewers: the model surfaces likely issues and refactors, humans validate correctness and tests.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 团队介绍了一种结合 Claude 自动检查与人工审查的混合代码评审流程：模型提出潜在问题与重构建议，人工负责验证正确性与测试。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Claude Code team describes a hybrid code-review workflow that combines automated checks from Claude with human reviewers: the model surfaces likely issues and refactors, humans validate correctness and tests. The process speeds reviews and catches routine problems, but relies on human oversight to avoid model errors and ensure design intent.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 团队介绍了一种结合 Claude 自动检查与人工审查的混合代码评审流程：模型提出潜在问题与重构建议，人工负责验证正确性与测试。该流程能加快审查并发现常见问题，但仍依赖人工把关以避免模型失误并保障设计意图。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.niemanlab.org/2026/10/metas-new-commercial-terms-will-allow-the-scraping-of-news-sites-legal-experts-say/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Meta’s new commercial terms will allow the scraping of news sites: experts</span><span class='zh'>Meta 新的商业条款将允许抓取新闻网站：专家观点</span></a><span class="dom">niemanlab.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Meta&#x27;s updated commercial terms open a path for scraping news sites to train models and power AI capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 更新的商业条款可能为新闻网站数据抓取用于训练模型铺平道路。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meta&#x27;s updated commercial terms open a path for scraping news sites to train models and power AI capabilities. Legal experts weigh the policy&#x27;s potential to seed large-scale data corpora against copyright and licensing concerns, and publishers&#x27; revenue models. The change could recalibrate who pays for access and how data ecosystems evolve.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 更新的商业条款可能为新闻网站数据抓取用于训练模型铺平道路。专家认为这或将推动大规模数据集的出现，同时引发版权、许可与出版商收入方面的担忧，或迫使数据访问和定价模式重估。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Regulatory staff were instructed to retract analysis of suspicious oil trades, suggesting external pressure influencing enforcement work. The episode highlights concerns about independence and transparency in market surveillance and regulatory credibility.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，美国商品期货交易委员会的工作人员被指示撤回对可疑石油交易的分析，显示监管工作可能受到外部压力的影响。此事引发对执法独立性、透明度与市场监管可信度的担忧。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Thunderbird is sharing a first look at a native iOS email app in development, signaling a major milestone for the open-source project.</span><span class='zh'>Thunderbird 正在展示其原生 iOS 邮件应用的初步样子，标志着这家开源项目的一大里程碑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Thunderbird is sharing a first look at a native iOS email app in development, signaling a major milestone for the open-source project. The iOS app aims to deliver a privacy-conscious, performant experience in the Apple ecosystem while staying aligned with Thunderbird&#x27;s cross-platform ethos.</span><span class='zh'>Thunderbird 正在展示其原生 iOS 邮件应用的初步样子，标志着这家开源项目的一大里程碑。iOS 版本计划在 Apple 生态中提供注重隐私、性能出色的邮件体验，同时坚持 Thunderbird 的跨平台理念。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/oct/06/google-datacentres-finland-temporary-halt-environmental-impact-assessment" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google told to halt work on datacentres in Finland over environmental concerns</span><span class='zh'>谷歌被要求暂停芬兰数据中心建设，因环境担忧</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Regulators ordered Google to halt construction of planned Finland datacenters pending an environmental impact assessment.</span><span class='zh'>监管机构要求谷歌暂停芬兰数据中心建设，直至完成环境影响评估。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Regulators ordered Google to halt construction of planned Finland datacenters pending an environmental impact assessment. The pause highlights growing scrutiny of data-center footprints in energy-rich regions and could affect Google&#x27;s regional cloud expansion timeline.</span><span class='zh'>监管机构要求谷歌暂停芬兰数据中心建设，直至完成环境影响评估。这一暂停凸显对数据中心环境足迹日益严格的审查，可能影响谷歌在该区域的云基础设施扩张计划。</span></p>

The move highlights tensions between device makers, platform APIs, and user expectations of seamless data sharing.</span><span class='zh'>Withings 对新用户推出 Apple Health 同步需要年费 99 美元的政策，引发关于互操作性的付费墙争议。这一举动凸显了设备厂商、平台 API 与用户对无缝数据共享期望之间的张力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.tomshardware.com/video-games/new-york-rep-announces-game-ownership-bill-that-would-require-consumer-ownership-rather-than-sale-of-licenses-would-also-require-publishers-to-keep-the-game-functional-after-support-ends" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>NY announces game ownership bill requiring ownership rather than licensing</span><span class='zh'>纽约宣布游戏所有权法案，要求消费者拥有游戏而非许可</span></a><span class="dom">tomshardware.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New York proposes a bill requiring consumer ownership of digital games rather than licenses and obligating publishers to keep titles functional after support ends.</span><span class='zh'>纽约提出的法案要求数字游戏以实际所有权形式转让给消费者，而不仅仅是许可，并要求发行商在停止支持后仍维持游戏功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New York proposes a bill requiring consumer ownership of digital games rather than licenses and obligating publishers to keep titles functional after support ends. The move could reshape digital rights, DRM debates, and post-sale software stewardship.</span><span class='zh'>纽约提出的法案要求数字游戏以实际所有权形式转让给消费者，而不仅仅是许可，并要求发行商在停止支持后仍维持游戏功能。此举可能重塑数字权利、数字版权管理与售后软件维护的格局。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>395</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50136536' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>178</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50041540" data-id="50041540" data-tags="Programming" data-score="393" data-comments="334" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50041540" data-time="1791713487">
<a class="thumb" href="https://ircv3.net/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=ircv3.net&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>ircv3.net</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">69</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ircv3.net/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>IRCv3</span><span class='zh'>IRCv3 规范</span></a><span class="dom">ircv3.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>IRCv3 defines modern extensions to the IRC protocol, enabling capability negotiation and richer metadata between clients and servers.</span><span class='zh'>IRCv3 定义了对 IRC 协议的现代化扩展，支持客户端与服务器之间的能力协商与更丰富的元数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>IRCv3 defines modern extensions to the IRC protocol, enabling capability negotiation and richer metadata between clients and servers. It aims to make IRC more usable and interoperable in a world of feature-rich clients. The spec underpins improvements like extended joins and better identity handling.</span><span class='zh'>IRCv3 定义了对 IRC 协议的现代化扩展，支持客户端与服务器之间的能力协商与更丰富的元数据。它旨在让 IRC 在功能丰富的客户端时代更易用、互操作性更强。该规范支撑了扩展加入、身份处理等改进。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New data shows UK electricity generation per person fallen well below 1970s levels, even as total output changes with policy shifts and energy efficiency. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/qgis/QGIS-Enhancement-Proposals/pull/387" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>QGIS enacts moratorium on AI contributions</span><span class='zh'>QGIS 对 AI 贡献实施暂停</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>QGIS has enacted a moratorium on accepting AI-generated contributions while governance and licensing concerns are assessed.</span><span class='zh'>QGIS 已暂停接受基于 AI 的贡献，原因在于对许可证、质量和滥用风险的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>QGIS has enacted a moratorium on accepting AI-generated contributions while governance and licensing concerns are assessed. The move signals open-source communities grappling with risk and quality control as AI tooling becomes more integrated. It may slow AI-assisted features in the GIS project but could set precedent for other OSS projects.</span><span class='zh'>QGIS 已暂停接受基于 AI 的贡献，原因在于对许可证、质量和滥用风险的担忧。此举显示开源社区在 AI 工具日益整合的环境下面临的风险与质量控制挑战，可能放慢该 GIS 项目中 AI 助力的功能部署，但也可能为其他开源项目树立先例。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Banks are trying to offload AI data-center debt they just underwrote, moving risk off their books.</span><span class='zh'>银行正试图处置他们刚承销的AI数据中心债务，将风险从自家账面转移出去。</span></p>

The move signals fragility in AI infrastructure financing and a potential shift toward securitization of tech debt as demand cools.</span><span class='zh'>银行正试图处置他们刚承销的AI数据中心债务，将风险从自家账面转移出去。这一举动暴露出AI基础设施融资的脆弱性，以及在需求降温时科技债务可能被证券化的趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.derekthompson.org/p/i-know-why-the-mathematicians-weep" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I know why the mathematicians weep</span><span class='zh'>我知道为什么数学家会哭</span></a><span class="dom">derekthompson.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Derek Thompson argues about the emotional and intellectual toll in mathematics, using history and anecdotes to explain why mathematicians weep.</span><span class='zh'>德里克·汤普森以历史和趣闻讲述数学的情感与智力代价，解释为何数学家会为难题而落泪。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Derek Thompson argues about the emotional and intellectual toll in mathematics, using history and anecdotes to explain why mathematicians weep. The piece links math’s beauty with its stubborn hard problems and reminds tech readers of the human side of theory.</span><span class='zh'>德里克·汤普森以历史和趣闻讲述数学的情感与智力代价，解释为何数学家会为难题而落泪。文章将数学之美与顽固难题联系起来，提醒科技读者理论背后的人性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reddit.com/r/sysadmin/comments/1x8cycd/namecheap_just_seized_and_disabled_all_my_domains/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Namecheap just seized and disabled all my domains</span><span class='zh'>Namecheap 竟然没收并禁用我所有的域名</span></a><span class="dom">reddit.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A user claims Namecheap seized and disabled all domains, raising questions about registrar control, ownership, and due process.</span><span class='zh'>用户称 Namecheap 竟然没收并禁用了他所有的域名，暴露了注册商对域名控制、所有权与流程的潜在风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A user claims Namecheap seized and disabled all domains, raising questions about registrar control, ownership, and due process.</span><span class='zh'>用户称 Namecheap 竟然没收并禁用了他所有的域名，暴露了注册商对域名控制、所有权与流程的潜在风险。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Archaeologists uncovered a royal archive from the Hittite civilization in Turkey, revealing tablets and inscriptions that illuminate ancient governance, diplomacy, and daily life.</span><span class='zh'>考古学家在土耳其发现了赫梯王朝的皇家档案，出土的泥板与铭文有助于揭示当时的统治、外交与日常生活。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Archaeologists uncovered a royal archive from the Hittite civilization in Turkey, revealing tablets and inscriptions that illuminate ancient governance, diplomacy, and daily life. The find could reshape understanding of Bronze Age Anatolia and offer new data for linguistics and history.</span><span class='zh'>考古学家在土耳其发现了赫梯王朝的皇家档案，出土的泥板与铭文有助于揭示当时的统治、外交与日常生活。此发现可能重塑对安纳托利亚青铜时代的认识，并为语言学和历史学提供新材料。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An exploration of selling vintage print issues of The Onion via an Archive Hunt-like platform, highlighting the quirks of media ephemera markets.</span><span class='zh'>探索通过类似 Archive Hunt 的平台出售《The Onion》的印刷旧刊，揭示媒体浮游文献市场的怪异现象。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An exploration of selling vintage print issues of The Onion via an Archive Hunt-like platform, highlighting the quirks of media ephemera markets. It touches on archiving culture, copyright questions, and the tension between physical artifacts and digital availability.</span><span class='zh'>探索通过类似 Archive Hunt 的平台出售《The Onion》的印刷旧刊，揭示媒体浮游文献市场的怪异现象。涉及档案文化、版权问题，以及实体与数字可用性之间的张力。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A community port brings llama.cpp to jailbroken PS5 consoles, enabling native inference of GGUF-format models directly on the console hardware. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI coding agents can generate more code, but this doesn&#x27;t translate into more usable software due to quality and integration hurdles.</span><span class='zh'>AI 编码代理确实能产生更多代码，但由于质量、模块化和集成方面的挑战，未能带来更多可用软件。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.readtheprofile.com/p/elizabeth-holmes-documentary-you-can-see-everything" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Elizabeth Holmes documentary “You Can See Everything” is deceiving</span><span class='zh'>伊丽莎白·霍尔姆斯纪录片《You Can See Everything》为何会产生误导</span></a><span class="dom">readtheprofile.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Critics argue the documentary leverages selective storytelling that omits crucial context and data, painting Theranos in a more favorable light than the full record warrants.</span><span class='zh'>评论者认为该纪录片采用选择性叙事，省略关键背景与数据，对 Theranos 的描绘比完整记录更有利。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Critics argue the documentary leverages selective storytelling that omits crucial context and data, painting Theranos in a more favorable light than the full record warrants. The piece invites viewers to scrutinize business documentaries for balance and truth, especially in biotech and startup narratives.</span><span class='zh'>评论者认为该纪录片采用选择性叙事，省略关键背景与数据，对 Theranos 的描绘比完整记录更有利。该文章呼吁观众对科技与创业题材的纪录片保持批判性，关注叙事平衡与真实之间的差距。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>While attempting to download an ISO, the author ends up discovering and crafting a kernel exploit, turning a routine download into hands-on vulnerability research.</span><span class='zh'>本篇讲述在尝试下载 ISO 时，作者意外发现并构建了一个内核漏洞利用，把常规下载变成了对安全问题的实战研究。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>While attempting to download an ISO, the author ends up discovering and crafting a kernel exploit, turning a routine download into hands-on vulnerability research. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://chillphysicsenjoyer.substack.com/p/making-chicken-transparent-using" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Making chicken transparent using Dorito dust</span><span class='zh'>用多力多滋粉让鸡肉变透明</span></a><span class="dom">chillphysicsenjoyer.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A quirky optics experiment uses Dorito cheese powder to illustrate light-scattering effects on a chicken sample.</span><span class='zh'>一个离奇的光学实验用多力多滋奶酪粉来演示鸡肉样本的光散射效应。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A quirky optics experiment uses Dorito cheese powder to illustrate light-scattering effects on a chicken sample. The setup highlights how additive powders can alter visibility under certain lighting, offering a playful take on visualization of internal structure. It&#x27;s a novelty demonstration rather than a cooking technique.</span><span class='zh'>一个离奇的光学实验用多力多滋奶酪粉来演示鸡肉样本的光散射效应。该装置展示了添加剂粉末在特定光照下如何改变可见度，提供了对内部结构可视化的趣味演示。它不是做饭技巧，而是对光学原理的趣味展示。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.sammystraus.com/#i-posted-to-hn-and-then-my-website-was-flooded-with-ai-bot-traffic" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I posted to HN, and then my website was flooded with AI bot traffic</span><span class='zh'>我在 Hacker News 发帖后，网站突然被 AI 机器人流量淹没</span></a><span class="dom">sammystraus.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Posting on Hacker News triggered a flood of automated AI bot traffic to the site, overwhelming servers and analytics.</span><span class='zh'>在 Hacker News 发帖后，网站突然遭遇大量 AI 机器人流量，导致服务器和分析工具被压垮。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Posting on Hacker News triggered a flood of automated AI bot traffic to the site, overwhelming servers and analytics. The incident highlights how quickly a link can attract mass bot activity and the need for basic protections like rate limiting and bot detection when you go viral. It also offers a case study in resilience for small sites facing sudden traffic bursts.</span><span class='zh'>在 Hacker News 发帖后，网站突然遭遇大量 AI 机器人流量，导致服务器和分析工具被压垮。事件凸显热门链接可能迅速吸引大规模自动访问，以及小站点在流量激增时需要的限流与机器人识别等防护措施。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.smithsonianmag.com/science-nature/dam-removals-are-bringing-fish-back-across-the-northeastern-united-states-striking-down-one-tiny-barrier-at-a-time-180989679/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dam removals are bringing fish back across the northeastern United States</span><span class='zh'>水坝拆除让东北部地区的鱼类回归</span></a><span class="dom">smithsonianmag.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Removing dams across the northeastern United States is restoring river connectivity, enabling migratory fish to repopulate historic habitats.</span><span class='zh'>拆除水坝正在重新打开河道连通性，让回游性鱼类重新占据历史栖息地，提升生态与渔业收益。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Removing dams across the northeastern United States is restoring river connectivity, enabling migratory fish to repopulate historic habitats. The restorations boost ecosystem health and fisheries, though results vary by river and ongoing management is needed.</span><span class='zh'>拆除水坝正在重新打开河道连通性，让回游性鱼类重新占据历史栖息地，提升生态与渔业收益。成效因河流而异，需持续监测与治理。</span></p>

The update signals ongoing experimentation and invites feedback on new capabilities, even if some features may be unstable.</span><span class='zh'>Jev 1.14.0 已发布，并附带面向早期尝试者的实验性功能。此次更新显示出持续探索新特性的意图，并鼓励用户提供反馈，尽管部分功能可能不稳定。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">100</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mortoray.com/why-arent-ai-companies-being-charged-for-hacking/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why aren&#x27;t AI companies being charged for hacking?</span><span class='zh'>为什么 AI 公司没有因为黑客行为被起诉？</span></a><span class="dom">mortoray.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post argues that charging AI companies for hacking is structurally difficult: attribution, intent, and the role of autonomous agents muddle liability, and current laws don’t map cleanly onto AI-enabled wrongdoing.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，针对AI公司因黑客行为提起指控在法律上存在结构性困难：归责、意图与自动化代理的作用使责任难以界定，现行法难以直接套用到AI相关的违法行为。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post argues that charging AI companies for hacking is structurally difficult: attribution, intent, and the role of autonomous agents muddle liability, and current laws don’t map cleanly onto AI-enabled wrongdoing. Until regulators clarify what counts as negligence or complicity, prosecutions against AI firms remain rare even when their tech is involved in breaches; the piece calls for clearer accountability frameworks to avoid chilling innovation.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，针对AI公司因黑客行为提起指控在法律上存在结构性困难：归责、意图与自动化代理的作用使责任难以界定，现行法难以直接套用到AI相关的违法行为。除非监管框架明确企业在何种情形承担共犯或疏忽责任，否则相关起诉往往难以推进。作者呼吁建立更清晰的问责规则，以避免对创新造成过度约束。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>329</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50057278' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>363</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50141076" data-id="50141076" data-tags="Open Source|Hardware" data-score="319" data-comments="51" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141076" data-time="1792331328">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/VikOlliver/RepRapMicron/wiki" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>RepRapMicron</span><span class='zh'>RepRapMicron：开放源代码的微型3D打印平台</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>RepRapMicron documents an open-source micro-scale motion platform for RepRap-style 3D systems, emphasizing sub-micrometer precision.</span><span class='zh'>RepRapMicron 记录了一个用于 RepRap 系列的微型尺度运动平台，强调亚纳米级（亚微米）精度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>RepRapMicron documents an open-source micro-scale motion platform for RepRap-style 3D systems, emphasizing sub-micrometer precision. The project offers mechanical designs, electronics, and firmware to enable precision micro-manufacturing at a hobbyist level. It showcases how open hardware can democratize advanced fabrication capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>RepRapMicron 记录了一个用于 RepRap 系列的微型尺度运动平台，强调亚纳米级（亚微米）精度。该项目提供机械设计、电子和固件，面向爱好者层面的微型制造。它展示了开源硬件如何让高端加工能力走向大众。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/snuri00/ps2-web-recomp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: PS2 games statically recompiled to WebAssembly</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：PS2 游戏静态重新编译为 WebAssembly</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN: A project that statically recompiles PlayStation 2 games to WebAssembly, making them runnable in browsers without dynamic emulation layers.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：一个项目将 PS2 游戏静态重新编译为 WebAssembly，使其在浏览器中无需动态模拟就能运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN: A project that statically recompiles PlayStation 2 games to WebAssembly, making them runnable in browsers without dynamic emulation layers. The approach demonstrates how modern toolchains can port legacy binaries with performance trade-offs and prompts discussion about the future of browser-based retro gaming.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：一个项目将 PS2 游戏静态重新编译为 WebAssembly，使其在浏览器中无需动态模拟就能运行。这一方法展示了现代工具链在移植旧体制二进制时的潜力与性能权衡，也引发对浏览器内复古游戏未来的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Saffsanity/sill" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Any window from your Mac, on your iPhone and iPad</span><span class='zh'>让 Mac 任意窗口出现在 iPhone 与 iPad 上</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Any window from your Mac, on your iPhone and iPad refers to a cross-device window mirroring tool enabling users to view or interact with a Mac window on iOS devices.</span><span class='zh'>一款让 Mac 任意窗口出现在 iPhone 与 iPad 上的工具正在展现跨设备协作的新方向，尽管仍处于实验阶段。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Any window from your Mac, on your iPhone and iPad refers to a cross-device window mirroring tool enabling users to view or interact with a Mac window on iOS devices. The project showcases a path toward seamless multi-device workflows, though early-stage and experimental. It hints at a future where a single desktop session spans the Apple ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>一款让 Mac 任意窗口出现在 iPhone 与 iPad 上的工具正在展现跨设备协作的新方向，尽管仍处于实验阶段。该项目凸显了未来可能的无缝多设备工作流，但在实际应用前还需克服稳定性与性能挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/home-assistant/home-assistant.io/issues/49314" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I never authorized an integration that scans my secrets</span><span class='zh'>我从未授权的集成在扫描我的秘密</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rumors or a complaint surfaced that a Home Assistant integration scanned user secrets without consent.</span><span class='zh'>有用户在 Home Assistant 的问题跟踪中声称某个集成在未获得授权的情况下扫描秘密信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rumors or a complaint surfaced that a Home Assistant integration scanned user secrets without consent. The incident highlights privacy risks in extensible platforms and the need for explicit consent and auditable secrets handling. It underscores the ongoing tension between platform openness and user data protection in open-source ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>有用户在 Home Assistant 的问题跟踪中声称某个集成在未获得授权的情况下扫描秘密信息。此事暴露了可扩展平台中的隐私风险，也强调需要对 secrets 的处理进行显式同意和可审计。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/antirez/c64-hw-simulator" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Commodore 64 hardware simulator: break chips and pins, see the results</span><span class='zh'>Commodore 64 硬件模拟器：解析芯片与引脚，观察结果</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Commodore 64 hardware simulator lets researchers poke at the system by &#x27;breaking&#x27; chips and pins to observe how signals propagate.</span><span class='zh'>这款 Commodore 64 硬件模拟器允许通过模拟对芯片与引脚的‘破坏’来观察信号如何在系统内传输。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Commodore 64 hardware simulator lets researchers poke at the system by &#x27;breaking&#x27; chips and pins to observe how signals propagate. The project illustrates hardware-level behavior and can aid understanding of retro systems, fault tolerance, and emulation accuracy.</span><span class='zh'>这款 Commodore 64 硬件模拟器允许通过模拟对芯片与引脚的‘破坏’来观察信号如何在系统内传输。该项目有助于理解硬件层面的行为、复古系统的仿真精度，以及对容错设计的研究。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Doory is a community-sourced, open-source design tool aiming to rival Canva, likely featuring templates, drag-and-drop editing, and export options.</span><span class='zh'>Doory 是一个开源的设计工具，旨在成为 Canva 的开源替代，具备模板、拖拽编辑和导出等功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Doory is a community-sourced, open-source design tool aiming to rival Canva, likely featuring templates, drag-and-drop editing, and export options. It represents a privacy-friendly, self-hostable alternative for teams and individuals who want control over their design pipeline.</span><span class='zh'>Doory 是一个开源的设计工具，旨在成为 Canva 的开源替代，具备模板、拖拽编辑和导出等功能。它为注重隐私、希望自托管设计流程的团队与个人提供了一个可控的替代选项。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/valimised/ivxv" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Source code of the Estonian online voting system</span><span class='zh'>爱沙尼亚在线投票系统源代码公开</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The source code of Estonia’s online voting platform has been released publicly, enabling independent audits and security research. This openness can improve transparency and help identify vulnerabilities, though online voting remains a debated topic.</span><span class='zh'>爱沙尼亚在线投票系统的源代码被公开，便于独立审计与安全研究。这一举措有望提升透明度并帮助发现潜在漏洞，但在线投票的安全性与隐私性仍是讨论焦点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/prabanta-dev/janas" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Janas: Run LLMs larger than RAM by streaming experts from SSD</span><span class='zh'>Janas：通过从 SSD 流式加载专家参数，在 RAM 之外运行超大规模 LLM</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Janas enables running LLMs larger than available RAM by streaming model components from SSD as needed.</span><span class='zh'>Janas 通过按需从 SSD 流式加载模型部分来在内存超限的情况下运行超大规模的 LLM。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Janas enables running LLMs larger than available RAM by streaming model components from SSD as needed. By using a mixture-of-experts design, it fetches parts of the model only when required, reducing memory pressure without needing massive hardware upgrades. The approach could lower the bar for experimenting with large models on commodity machines.</span><span class='zh'>Janas 通过按需从 SSD 流式加载模型部分来在内存超限的情况下运行超大规模的 LLM。它利用专家模型的分布式架构，只有在需要时才获取相应参数，降低对内存的压力，无需巨额硬件升级。该思路若规模化，将降低在普通硬件上尝试大模型的门槛。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>219</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50063717' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>93</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50086165" data-id="50086165" data-tags="Programming|Data" data-score="206" data-comments="52" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50086165" data-time="1791978473">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/timescale/pg_vectorsearch" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pg_vectorsearch: An index that is faster than pgvector&#x27;s IVFFlat and HNSW</span><span class='zh'>Pg_vectorsearch：比 pgvector 的 IVFFlat 和 HNSW 更快的索引</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pg_vectorsearch is a PostgreSQL extension that claims faster vector-index performance than pgvector&#x27;s IVFFlat and HNSW implementations, targeting lower-latency nearest-neighbor queries inside the database.</span><span class='zh'>Pg_vectorsearch 是一个 PostgreSQL 扩展，宣称在向量索引性能上比 pgvector 的 IVFFlat 和 HNSW 更快，重点是降低数据库内的近邻查询延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pg_vectorsearch is a PostgreSQL extension that claims faster vector-index performance than pgvector&#x27;s IVFFlat and HNSW implementations, targeting lower-latency nearest-neighbor queries inside the database. Embedding an optimized index into Postgres can simplify architecture by avoiding external vector stores while improving search speed for vector-heavy workloads.</span><span class='zh'>Pg_vectorsearch 是一个 PostgreSQL 扩展，宣称在向量索引性能上比 pgvector 的 IVFFlat 和 HNSW 更快，重点是降低数据库内的近邻查询延迟。把高效索引嵌入 Postgres 可以减少对外部向量存储的依赖，同时为向量密集型负载提升检索速度。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>206</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50086165' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>52</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50131189" data-id="50131189" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Hardware|Web|Education" data-score="186" data-comments="39" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131189" data-time="1792245068">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mikroverk/esp32sim" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Esp32sim – Run ESP32 firmware in the browser</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Esp32sim—在浏览器中运行 ESP32 固件</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Esp32sim lets you run ESP32 firmware directly in the browser, enabling rapid prototyping and education without hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Esp32sim 让你在浏览器中直接运行 ESP32 固件，降低硬件接入门槛，便于快速原型和教学。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Esp32sim lets you run ESP32 firmware directly in the browser, enabling rapid prototyping and education without hardware. It leverages browser-based emulation to emulate the ESP32 environment, reducing setup friction for developers and students. A practical tool for hands-on learning and quick debugging.</span><span class='zh'>Esp32sim 让你在浏览器中直接运行 ESP32 固件，降低硬件接入门槛，便于快速原型和教学。它通过浏览器端的仿真实现 ESP32 环境的模拟，减少开发者和学生的搭建成本。是一个面向动手学习与快速调试的实用工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/0x23/MicroManipulatorStepper" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Open MicroManipulator – A sub-micrometer 3D motion control plattform</span><span class='zh'>Open MicroManipulator：亚微米级三维运动控制平台</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Open MicroManipulator is a sub-micrometer 3D motion control platform with open hardware and firmware.</span><span class='zh'>Open MicroManipulator 提供一个开源硬件与固件的亚微米级三维运动控制平台，旨在让研究与教育领域的高精度操作民主化，便于实验室和创客空间实现极细微的控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Open MicroManipulator is a sub-micrometer 3D motion control platform with open hardware and firmware. The project aims to democratize precision manipulation for research and education, enabling ultra-fine control in labs and makerspaces.</span><span class='zh'>Open MicroManipulator 提供一个开源硬件与固件的亚微米级三维运动控制平台，旨在让研究与教育领域的高精度操作民主化，便于实验室和创客空间实现极细微的控制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/colinhacks/zod/commit/d821685ea6732436fcdf786a81d076958471e467" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Zod 4.6.6 compromised by malware?</span><span class='zh'>Zod 4.6.6 是否被恶意软件入侵？</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A suspected malware compromise affected a Zod release, raising concerns about supply-chain security for popular open-source libraries.</span><span class='zh'>对流行开源库 Zod 的发行版本怀疑被嵌入恶意软件，引发对软件供应链安全的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A suspected malware compromise affected a Zod release, raising concerns about supply-chain security for popular open-source libraries. The incident underscores the need for reproducible builds, vendor transparency, and rapid incident response in the ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>对流行开源库 Zod 的发行版本怀疑被嵌入恶意软件，引发对软件供应链安全的担忧。事件凸显了可复现构建、供应商透明度和快速事件响应在生态系统中的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/25921" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Codex Desktop continuously generates Crashpad pending dumps</span><span class='zh'>Codex Desktop 不断生成 Crashpad 待处理转储</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Codex Desktop keeps generating Crashpad pending dumps, indicating a crash-reporting flow bug that could waste disk space or raise privacy concerns.</span><span class='zh'>Codex Desktop 不断生成 Crashpad 待处理转储，表明崩溃报告流程存在缺陷，可能造成磁盘空间浪费或隐私风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Codex Desktop keeps generating Crashpad pending dumps, indicating a crash-reporting flow bug that could waste disk space or raise privacy concerns. Maintainers are tracking the issue and discussing fixes and mitigations.</span><span class='zh'>Codex Desktop 不断生成 Crashpad 待处理转储，表明崩溃报告流程存在缺陷，可能造成磁盘空间浪费或隐私风险。维护者正在跟进并讨论修复与缓解措施。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rogerbinns/apsw" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>apsw: Another Python SQLite Wrapper</span><span class='zh'>apsw：又一个 Python SQLite 封装器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>APSW offers a Python SQLite wrapper with lower-level access and extra features beyond the standard interface.</span><span class='zh'>APSW 是一个为 Python 提供 SQLite 封装的库，提供比内置 sqlite3 更低层次的访问和扩展功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>APSW offers a Python SQLite wrapper with lower-level access and extra features beyond the standard interface. It exposes advanced APIs for virtual tables, custom collations, and direct blob handling, appealing to power users who need fine-grained control—though it requires more maintenance.</span><span class='zh'>APSW 是一个为 Python 提供 SQLite 封装的库，提供比内置 sqlite3 更低层次的访问和扩展功能。它支持高级 API，如虚拟表、定制排序以及直接操作 BLOB，适合需要更细粒度控制的高级开发者；不过使用起来需要更多手工维护和编码约束。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/bealesh/neckbeard" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Neckbeard – A DevOps/platform/SRE plugin for your agent</span><span class='zh'>Neckbeard——为你的代理提供的 DevOps/平台/SRE 插件</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Neckbeard is a plugin collection that integrates agent workflows with DevOps, platform, and SRE tasks—think CI/CD interactions, infrastructure control, and observability hooks for agent-driven automation.</span><span class='zh'>Neckbeard 是一组插件，旨在将代理工作流与 DevOps、平台和 SRE 任务集成，包括 CI/CD、基础设施控制和可观测性接口。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Neckbeard is a plugin collection that integrates agent workflows with DevOps, platform, and SRE tasks—think CI/CD interactions, infrastructure control, and observability hooks for agent-driven automation. It aims to make agents more useful and safer in operational contexts by providing ready-made connectors and operational primitives.</span><span class='zh'>Neckbeard 是一组插件，旨在将代理工作流与 DevOps、平台和 SRE 任务集成，包括 CI/CD、基础设施控制和可观测性接口。它通过提供现成的连接器和操作原语，使代理在运行时管理场景中更实用且更安全。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ClickHouse/clickhouse-c" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ClickHouse C – Talk to ClickHouse using its native binary protocol</span><span class='zh'>ClickHouse C：用原生二进制协议与 ClickHouse 通信</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Announces a C library that talks to ClickHouse using the database&#x27;s native binary protocol, reducing overhead and simplifying integration from C applications.</span><span class='zh'>推出一个用于 C 的 ClickHouse 客户端库，通过原生二进制协议与 ClickHouse 交互，降低开销并简化在 C 应用中的集成，提升数据写入与查询效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Announces a C library that talks to ClickHouse using the database&#x27;s native binary protocol, reducing overhead and simplifying integration from C applications. This streamlines high-performance data ingestion and querying for C ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>推出一个用于 C 的 ClickHouse 客户端库，通过原生二进制协议与 ClickHouse 交互，降低开销并简化在 C 应用中的集成，提升数据写入与查询效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/graphicle-ai/graphicle" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Graphicle, a Rust port of Graphify with memory for coding agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Graphicle——Graphify 的 Rust 移植，带有编码代理记忆</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Graphicle is a Rust port of Graphify that adds a memory model tailored for coding agents, enabling them to retain context and past decisions during multi-step programming tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Graphicle 是 Graphify 的 Rust 移植版，增加了为编码代理设计的记忆模型，使代理在多步骤编程任务中能保留上下文与历史决策。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Graphicle is a Rust port of Graphify that adds a memory model tailored for coding agents, enabling them to retain context and past decisions during multi-step programming tasks. Implementing this in Rust emphasizes performance and safety, making it attractive for systems that need reliable, low-latency agent-assisted coding workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Graphicle 是 Graphify 的 Rust 移植版，增加了为编码代理设计的记忆模型，使代理在多步骤编程任务中能保留上下文与历史决策。用 Rust 实现强调性能与安全，适合需要可靠、低延迟代理辅助编码的系统。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/hibanaworks/hibana" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Choreographic Programming in Rust</span><span class='zh'>Rust 的编舞式编程</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Choreographic programming applies global specifications to coordinate distributed components.</span><span class='zh'>舞蹈式编程通过全局规范来协调分布式组件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Choreographic programming applies global specifications to coordinate distributed components. The Rust project demonstrates how to express choreography as code, enabling safer composition of interacting modules. It offers a path to modeling concurrent systems with formal constraints while leveraging Rust&#x27;s type system.</span><span class='zh'>舞蹈式编程通过全局规范来协调分布式组件。该 Rust 项目展示了将编排作为代码书写，从而实现对交互模块的更安全组合。此方向为用形式化约束建模并发系统提供了一条利用 Rust 类型系统的路线。</span></p>
